**Exit criteria**
- At least 25 programs (11-programs plus the M9 struct and function-pointer tests) pass at -O0 and -O2 with the native oracle required.
- Legs green: .NET 8 (tiered and TieredCompilation=0), .NET 10, .NET Framework 4.8 (Windows), WSL .NET 10 with x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil (tiered and TieredCompilation=0), Linux ilasm inside WSL, modern Windows ilasm, ildasm round trip.
- msil-verify (ILVerify allow-list + PEVerify), jitall and MSILStackCheck report no stack-shape errors.
- The local gate runs the same msil/utils/ci scripts as msil-ci.yml; enabling the workflow remains the user's decision.
- Documentation committed, including the 'Proposed Design.md updates' list.

**Review focus** (in addition to the three standing lenses)
- Programs are UB-free and meaningful (native -O0/-O2 agree); coverage maps to every Design.md 12 example.
- Verification policy is enforced, not advisory; allow-list unchanged or justified.
- Docs match behaviour; Design.md untouched (proposals only).

**Rebuild cost.** MSIL target TUs + relink if fixes are needed: ~5-15 min; the full lit legs take a few minutes.

### M11. Upstream-merge follow-ups: crash and pipeline fixes, upstream-hook coverage, more gate legs

**Goal.** Close the ten items that the upstream merge of 2026-10-19 deferred (section 12 row 'Deferred after the upstream merge'; the numbers (1)-(10) below are that row's). Late function splitting no longer crashes the MSIL AsmPrinter (3); `llc -stop-after=msil-isel` and `-stop-before=msil-argument-move` print MIR that passes the machine verifier and reads back (4); LowerInvoke runs once and UnreachableBlockElim no longer runs a second time after CodeGenPrepare (5). Independent tests pin the aarch64 aggregate signatures that upstream de7794fcf781 changed (1), the MC assembly parser's IsMSIL hook (6) and `-mllvm` options through clang (10). A curated 'upstream-hooks' check runs the upstream regression tests of every upstream file the fork changes, proves that its list of hooked files is complete, and compiles the two hooked files that this build never compiles in a private build tree on C: (6, 7). actions/upload-artifact is pinned as upstream pins it, and a checker keeps every action pin in step after later merges (2). run-utc-tests.py (8) and the -O1/-O3/-Os/-Oz execution levels (9) become regular gate and CI legs. Documentation follows. No IL changes for any input that compiled before. The user decided on 2026-10-20 to do this before Phase 2 planning (section 12, row 'M11 added').

**Depends on:** M10 and the upstream merge 2ea3fa74b232 (section 12, 2026-10-19). Before the pre-spawn checks the orchestrator copies the recon evidence of 2026-10-20 to `llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/` (section 12, row 'M11 added'); every Reads entry below that names `llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/` points there.

**Header changes:**
- none (MSILUtilities.h is target-local: the 13 MSIL TUs that include it today and MSILISelDAGToDAG.cpp, which starts to, recompile)

**Work packages**

#### M11.a Function splitting off, verifier-clean instruction selection, one invoke lowering (implement, ~120 lines)

- Owned files: `llvm/lib/Target/MSIL/MSILTargetMachine.cpp`, `llvm/lib/Target/MSIL/MSILISelDAGToDAG.cpp`, `llvm/lib/Target/MSIL/MSILArgumentMove.cpp`, `llvm/lib/Target/MSIL/MSILUtilities.h`, `llvm/lib/Target/MSIL/MSILUtilities.cpp`
- Reads: llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split.md sections 0-7 and 10; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split-files/item3.diff; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split-files/tools/ (relink.ps1, mkrsp.py); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/stop-after.md sections 1-9 and 12; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/stop-after-files/ (prototype.diff, compile.ps1, link.ps1); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/eh-passes.md sections 1-7 and 10; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/eh-passes-files/tools/ (build.ps1, ehcount.py, corpus.py, ildiff.py); llvm/lib/CodeGen/TargetPassConfig.cpp (constructor ~440-475, addPassesToHandleExceptions ~767-811, addMachinePasses ~1105-1160); llvm/lib/CodeGen/BasicBlockSections.cpp (~470-530, ~578-581); llvm/lib/CodeGen/CodeGenTargetMachineImpl.cpp (~240-250); llvm/lib/Target/WebAssembly/WebAssemblyTargetMachine.cpp (its addPassesToHandleExceptions override); llvm/lib/Target/WebAssembly/WebAssemblyArgumentMove.cpp; this plan decisions D8, D23, D30, D33 and sections 5.3, 5.4
- Provides: `bool MSIL::moveArgumentsToTop(MachineFunction &MF)` (MSILUtilities.h: moves every ARGUMENT_<RC> of the entry block above its first other instruction, keeping the relative order of both groups; true if an instruction moved); `void MSILPassConfig::addPassesToHandleExceptions() override` (adds no pass); the MSILPassConfig constructor forces `TM.Options.FunctionSplitting = FunctionSplittingMode::None`

Three independent fixes; the recon reports hold the reproductions, the root causes with file:line references, the rejected alternatives and verified prototypes. (1) Item 3, function splitting. TargetPassConfig's constructor applies llc's and `-mllvm`'s `-function-splitting=` to `TM.Options` after the TargetMachine exists (TargetPassConfig.cpp ~472-473), and cc1 `-fsplit-machine-functions` sets the mode before (clang BackendUtil.cpp ~432-433). With `All`, addMachinePasses adds BasicBlockSections with SplitWithPGO, which gives a profiled function (every function under `-mfs-split-ehcode`) section type Preset; the AsmPrinter then switches to the nullptr section that MSILTargetObjectFile inherits, and MCStreamer asserts 'Cannot switch to a null section!'. Give the MSILPassConfig constructor a body that sets `TM.Options.FunctionSplitting = FunctionSplittingMode::None;` with the comment of split.md section 7 (CIL methods have no sections, so no function is ever split; dropped silently like the basic block sections in the MSILTargetMachine constructor (D8), but here, because the base constructor has just applied the command-line mode and addMachinePasses reads it when it builds the pipeline). A reset in the MSILTargetMachine constructor alone leaves llc and `-mllvm` crashing (verified); do not add one there. No header change (FunctionSplittingMode is in llvm/Target/TargetOptions.h, already included) and no clang change: the driver already rejects `-fsplit-machine-functions`, `-fbasic-block-sections=all|list=`, `-fbasic-block-address-map` and `-fpartition-static-data-sections` on every MSIL triple, because their object format is not ELF (`-fbasic-block-sections=labels`, a deprecated alias, passes the driver as `-fbasic-block-address-map`, which MSILPrepareIR already ignores with a warning). Do not add section hooks to MSILTargetObjectFile or an `isFunctionSafeToSplit` override (split.md section 6, options D and E: BasicBlockSections would still run after RegStackify, ExplicitLocals and RegNumbering). (2) Item 4, stop points. Upstream 663669080864 makes llc run the machine verifier 'At end of pipeline' whenever it stops before code emission, and the MIR parser verifies on read. Every MSIL instruction carries `implicit-def dead $arguments`, ARGUMENT_<RC> reads `implicit $arguments`, and the ScheduleDAG scheduler can put an ARGUMENT after another instruction, which the verifier reports as 'Using an undefined physical register'. Make the output of instruction selection clean (stop-after.md option A, section 9; the recon's inline prototype is stop-after-files/prototype.diff): move the static `argumentMove` of MSILArgumentMove.cpp into MSILUtilities as `MSIL::moveArgumentsToTop` (declared next to `getCopyOpcodeForRegClass`, with a `class MachineFunction;` forward declaration if the header lacks one; use `llvm::make_early_inc_range` for the second loop); in `MSILDAGToDAGISel::runOnMachineFunction` keep the `MF.setUseDebugInstrRef(false)` call, call `SelectionDAGISel::runOnMachineFunction(MF)`, then `MSIL::moveArgumentsToTop(MF)`, and return the base's result, with a comment naming the scheduler, the end-of-pipeline verifier (663669080864) and the MIR parser's verify-on-read, and include "MSILUtilities.h"; `MSILArgumentMoveLegacy::runOnMachineFunction` keeps its LLVM_DEBUG banner and returns `MSIL::moveArgumentsToTop(MF)`, and the file comment says that instruction selection already moves the ARGUMENTs and that the pass stays in the pipeline (plan 5.3 and pipeline.ll unchanged) for MIR input such as reg-argument.mir. The DBG_VALUEs that SelectionDAGISel puts after each ARGUMENT stay where they are, so they follow the moved ARGUMENTs (M11.b tests a debug-info function). Do not change the .td operand lists (plan 5.4 keeps `implicit-def dead $arguments`) or MSILRegisterInfo (ARGUMENTS stays unreserved), and do not use FailsVerification or `-disable-mir-output-verify` (stop-after.md section 7, options B-F). (3) Item 5, invoke lowering. MSILPassConfig::addIRPasses lowers invokes early (the WebAssembly no-EH shape copied in M2), and TargetPassConfig::addPassesToHandleExceptions adds LowerInvoke and UnreachableBlockElim again after CodeGenPrepare for ExceptionHandling::None; upstream fa24f6dba7cd made that hook virtual. Declare `void addPassesToHandleExceptions() override;` after `void addIRPasses() override;` in class MSILPassConfig and define it after MSILPassConfig::addIRPasses with no addPass call, the comment of eh-passes.md section 7 (the exception model is always None, D8; addIRPasses has already lowered every invoke into a call and removed the landing pads that became unreachable, before the target and generic IR passes; TargetPassConfig would add LowerInvoke and UnreachableBlockElim a second time after CodeGenPrepare) and its assert that `TM->getMCAsmInfo().getExceptionHandlingType()` is `ExceptionHandling::None` (add `#include "llvm/MC/MCAsmInfo.h"`). Keep the early LowerInvoke and UnreachableBlockElim in addIRPasses (WebAssembly's shape after fa24f6dba7cd; deleting them instead, option B, would let invokes and dead landing pads reach IndirectBrExpand, LSR and CodeGenPrepare) and extend their comment to say that addPassesToHandleExceptions adds nothing. Checks: msil-syntax.ps1 on the four changed .cpp files (MSILTargetMachine.cpp, MSILISelDAGToDAG.cpp, MSILArgumentMove.cpp, MSILUtilities.cpp) and on MSILUtilities.h; git-clang-format on the changed lines. The WP cannot build the shared tree, but it may verify with a private llc and clang as the recon did, never writing D:\llvm-msil\build: compile the four changed TUs into scratch with their compile_commands.json commands (output paths redirected, as stop-after-files/compile.ps1 does for one TU), then let split-files/tools/mkrsp.py build the llc and clang link lines with those objects ahead of the build tree's libraries and link with lld-link. split-files/tools/relink.ps1 compiles only MSILTargetMachine.cpp with hard-coded flags, so generalize it to the four TUs first. Then run the reproductions of split.md section 2, stop-after.md section 2 (with stop-after-files/run/dbg.c) and eh-passes.md section 2. The recon measured byte-identical IL on its whole corpus for all three fixes; any IL difference is a bug. llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/pipeline.ll, which pins the old double lowering, belongs to M11.b and fails until both WPs are in.

#### M11.b Tests for function splitting, the msil-isel stop point and the EH pass pipeline (test, ~450 lines)

- Owned files: `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/function-splitting.ll`, `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/stop-after-isel.ll`, `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/pipeline.ll`, `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/split-machine-functions.c`, `clang/test/Driver/msil-toolchain.c`
- Reads: llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split.md sections 1, 2, 4 and 8; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split-files/proposed-tests/ (validated texts); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split-files/repro/ (inputs); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/stop-after.md sections 2, 4 and 10; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/stop-after-files/suite/stop-after-isel.ll (validated text); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/stop-after-files/run/ (dbg.c, its -O2 IR and the MIR it gives after the fix); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/eh-passes.md sections 2 and 8; llvm/test/CodeGen/Generic/machine-function-splitter.ll (input model); llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/basic-block-sections.ll; llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/reg-argument.mir; llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/stack-protector-warning.ll; this plan section 2 (test conventions), section 5.4 (ARGUMENT operand list) and decision D8

Written from the specification only (this subsection and the recon test sections named in Reads); do not read the M11.a files. Plan section 2 conventions apply (both x86_64 MSIL triples, `-verify-machineinstrs`, a `%if ilasm` round trip where a module is complete). (1) Item 3. `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/function-splitting.ll` (new; validated text proposed-tests/function-splitting.ll, spec split.md section 8.1): a split-file input mfs.ll (internal noinline `@bar` and `@baz` defined in the module; `@foo1` with `!prof` function_entry_count 7000, branch weights 7000:0 and an InstrProf ProfileSummary module flag) and a basic-block-sections profile other.txt naming only `bar` ('v1' / 'f bar' / 'c 0'). For both triples: a baseline and an -O0 baseline with `llc -o`; then `-function-splitting=all`, `-function-splitting=all -O0`, `-function-splitting=all -mfs-split-ehcode`, `-function-splitting=all --basic-block-sections=all` and `-function-splitting=all --basic-block-sections=%t/other.txt`, each with `2>&1 | count 0` and a `diff` against the baseline of the same level; `-function-splitting=all --basic-block-address-map` with FileCheck of exactly the existing line 'warning: basic block address map is not supported for MSIL; ignored' and nothing else, plus a `diff`; a FileCheck of the baseline IL that shows the cold block (in foo1: brfalse to a label, `call int32 'bar'()`, ret, the label, `call int32 'baz'()`, ret); a pipeline check `llc -function-splitting=all --basic-block-sections=all -debug-pass=Structure ... 2>&1 | FileCheck --check-prefix=PIPE --implicit-check-not=bbsections --implicit-check-not="Basic Block Sections"` (`PIPE: Pass Arguments:`, `PIPE: MSIL Assembly Printer`) on both triples; the ilasm round trip of a split run; and a native control under `%if x86-registered-target` (`llc -mtriple=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu -function-splitting=all` prints `.section .text.split.foo1` and then `foo1.cold:`), so the input stays split-eligible after later upstream changes. Today it fails at the first `-function-splitting=all` line (assertion, exit status 0xc000001d). `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/split-machine-functions.c` (new, `// REQUIRES: msil-registered-target`; validated text proposed-tests/split-machine-functions.c, spec split.md section 8.2): the same IR without a triple in a split-file part; for both x86_64 triples a cc1 baseline (`%clang_cc1 -triple T -S -Wno-override-module -x ir`) with FileCheck of the bar and baz calls in foo1, then cc1 `-fsplit-machine-functions` and cc1 `-mllvm -function-splitting=all` (each `count 0` on stderr and a `diff` against the cc1 baseline), then a driver baseline (`%clang --target=T -S -Wno-override-module -x ir`) and the driver with `-Xclang -fsplit-machine-functions` (`count 0`, `diff` against the driver baseline, because the driver prints the Windows triple with its MSVC version), plus one `%if ilasm` round trip; no -O2 (the optimizer folds the calls away). Append to `clang/test/Driver/msil-toolchain.c` the driver guard of split.md section 8.3 (the RUN and check lines of proposed-tests/msil-section-options.c, and a sentence in the header comment). Its check prefixes (SPLIT, NOSPLIT, BBS-*, MAP, PSDS) are not used in msil-toolchain.c today; rename one only if it collides. Change the file name in the BBS-LIST pattern (`{{.*}}msil-section-options.c`) to `msil-toolchain.c`, because `%s` is now msil-toolchain.c. `not %clang -### --target=T -S <opt> %s` reports "error: unsupported option '<opt>' for target '<printed triple>'" for `-fsplit-machine-functions` (x86_64 Linux, MinGW, aarch64 Linux, and windows-msvc with `-fms-compatibility-version=19.40`, printed as `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc19.40.0-msil`), `-fbasic-block-sections=all` and `=list=%s` (x86_64 and aarch64 Linux), `-fbasic-block-address-map` (Linux, MinGW, msvc 19.40, aarch64) and `-fpartition-static-data-sections` (Linux); accepted are `-fsplit-machine-functions -fno-split-machine-functions`, `-fbasic-block-sections=none` and `-fbasic-block-sections=labels` (its deprecation warning, then `"-fbasic-block-address-map"` on the cc1 line); do not pin `-fno-basic-block-address-map` (upstream rejects it too). These lines pass today; they guard the upstream driver behaviour that MSIL relies on. (2) Item 4. `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/stop-after-isel.ll` (new; the validated text is stop-after-files/suite/stop-after-isel.ll, also in stop-after.md section 10): `@sel` (select on an i1 argument) and `@rotr_i64` (`llvm.fshr.i64(%a, %a, %b)`), whose schedules put an ARGUMENT after another instruction at -O0 and -O2; `-stop-after=msil-isel` at -O0 and -O2 on both triples into %t files, each FileChecked; `-stop-before=msil-argument-move` at -O2 on both triples piped to FileCheck; `-run-pass=none` on the -O2 outputs (verify on read); `-start-after=msil-isel` on the -O2 outputs compared with `diff` against the full pipeline's IL of the same triple; one `%if ilasm` round trip. CHECKs per function: `liveins: $arguments`, the blank line, then one CHECK-NEXT line per argument (`%{{[0-9]+}}:i32 = ARGUMENT_I32 {{[0-2]}}, implicit $arguments` three times for `@sel`, `%{{[0-9]+}}:i64 = ARGUMENT_I64 {{[01]}}, implicit $arguments` twice for `@rotr_i64`), then `CHECK-NOT: ARGUMENT_` up to `RETURN`; pin neither the order among the ARGUMENTs nor vreg numbers. The same file also has a debug-info function (required, not optional): the -O2 IR that clang emits with `-g` for stop-after-files/run/dbg.c's `sel` (`int sel(_Bool c, int a, int b) { return c ? a : b; }`), renamed `@sel_dbg`, with its debug records and metadata kept and the datalayout and triple removed. For it the CHECKs require the three ARGUMENT_I32 lines first (CHECK-NEXT after the blank line), then a `DBG_VALUE %{{[0-9]+}}, $noreg, !{{[0-9]+}}` line for each of the three arguments before `RETURN`, with no ARGUMENT_ line after them (stop-after-files/run/dbg.x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil-O2.mir shows the expected shape); the -O2 output of this function is part of the files that are read back with `-run-pass=none` and compiled on with `-start-after=msil-isel`. No RUN line passes `-disable-mir-output-verify` or `-verify-machineinstrs=0`, and the header says so. Today the first RUN line aborts in the 'At end of pipeline' verifier, and the debug-info function aborts at -O2 on its own. (3) Item 5. Amend `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/pipeline.ll` as eh-passes.md section 8 specifies: in all five RUN lines 'Lower invoke and unwind, for unwindless code generators' appears exactly once, after 'MSIL Fix Function Bitcasts' (after 'MSIL Optimize Returned' at -O2) and before 'Expand indirectbr instructions', and 'Remove unreachable blocks from the CFG' exactly twice (right after LowerInvoke, and after 'Shadow Stack GC Lowering'); neither appears after 'CodeGen Prepare'. Add `--implicit-check-not="Lower invoke and unwind"` and `--implicit-check-not="Remove unreachable blocks from the CFG"` to the `%{check}` DEFINE (the 'Pass Arguments' line spells them `-lowerinvoke` and `-unreachableblockelim`, which these case-sensitive strings do not match), replace the CHECK block with the seven lines of eh-passes.md section 8, and rewrite the header: the 'exceptions' bullet (the exception model is always None, D8, and MSILPassConfig::addPassesToHandleExceptions adds nothing, so LowerInvoke runs exactly once, in addIRPasses, and UnreachableBlockElim twice; no DWARF, SjLj or Windows EH preparation pass, even with `-exception-model=wineh`), the 'Every output line that names an MSIL pass must be matched' paragraph (it now also covers the two EH strings), and the D33 sentence (the pipeline was fixed in M2; M11 removed the second invoke lowering). Today the amended test fails with 'IMPLICIT-CHECK-NOT: excluded string found in input'. Run every RUN command by hand into scratch (concurrent lit runs share Output directories) and record in the hand-over that the item 3, 4 and 5 tests fail as described with the current shared build (they pass only after the gate builds M11.a) and that the msil-toolchain.c additions pass. Gate step 13 fails if one of these files, the debug-info case, the pipeline.ll implicit checks or the msil-toolchain.c guard is missing.

#### M11.c Tests pinning upstream changes: aarch64 aggregate signatures, the cc1as IsMSIL hook, -mllvm through clang (test, ~500 lines)

- Owned files: `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/aarch64-aggregate-abi.c`, `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/struct-args-aapcs64.ll`, `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/cc1as-unsupported.s`, `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/mllvm.c`
- Reads: llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs.md (items 1 and 10); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs-proto/ (aarch64-aggregate-abi.c, aarch64-agg-probe.c, mllvm.c); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/hooks.md section 3; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/hooks-artifacts/cc1as-unsupported.s; llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/struct-args-sysv.ll; llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/struct-args-win64.ll; llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/aarch64-triples.ll (the `-arm64` ilasm convention); llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/assembly-name.ll; clang/test/CodeGen/AArch64/args.cpp; clang/test/CodeGen/arm64-microsoft-arguments.cpp; clang/test/CodeGen/mllvm-library-options.ll; this plan sections 2 and 5.1 and decisions D2, D6, D28

Written from the specification only; these tests need no M11 implementation and must pass with the current shared build (run their commands by hand into scratch). If one exposes a compiler bug, report it with a reproducer: the fix goes through a fix-loop ownership grant (section 1.7a). (1) Item 1, aarch64 aggregates. Upstream de7794fcf781 passes a non-HFA aggregate argument of fewer than 8 bytes as `iN` of its exact size (i8 ... i56, formerly i64) on all four aarch64 MSIL triples, so 1-4-byte aggregates are now `int32` IL parameters and 5-7-byte ones stay `native int`; results did not change (legs.md item 1 has the full table). `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/aarch64-aggregate-abi.c` (new, `// REQUIRES: msil-registered-target`; prototype legs-proto/aarch64-aggregate-abi.c): the header comment says why the test exists; IR RUN lines `%clang_cc1 -triple <t> -emit-llvm -o - %s | FileCheck %s --check-prefixes=IR,IR-LNX` for aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil and aarch64-unknown-linux-musl-msil and `IR,IR-WIN` for aarch64-pc-windows-msvc-msil and aarch64-w64-windows-gnu-msil; IL RUN lines `%clang_cc1 -triple <t> -S -O2 -o %t.<x>.il %s` and `FileCheck %s --check-prefix=IL --implicit-check-not=int64 --input-file=%t.<x>.il` for all four triples, plus -O0 for the gnu and msvc triples; `%if ilasm %{ %ilasm-verify -arm64 -output=... %}` on the gnu -O2 and msvc -O0 outputs. One function per shape (structs of 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 12, 16 and 24 bytes, int+float, char+float, the HFAs float2, float3, double4, double1 and float[3], a pointer pair), each with an `IR-LABEL: define {{.*}}i24 @f3(i24 %x.coerce)`-style line (HFA rows: alignstack(8) on Linux only) and an exact `IL: {{^}}.method public static int32 'f3'(int32) cil managed{{$}}` line; call sites through noinline callees that store to a volatile global (`g3(struct S3)`, `g5(struct S5)`, `call_small`: `IR: call void @g3(i24`, `IL: call void 'g3'(int32)`, `IL: call void 'g5'(native int)`), because a `return x` callee is deleted at -O2; leave the vector_size HVAs out. `llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/struct-args-aapcs64.ll` (new, the AAPCS64 twin of struct-args-sysv.ll and struct-args-win64.ll): the IR that clang -O2 emits for aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil (generate it, then remove the datalayout and the triple), with callees taking and returning i8, i16, i24, i32, i40, i48, i56, i64, `[2 x i64]`, `[2 x ptr]`, `[2 x float] alignstack(8)`, `[4 x double] alignstack(8)`, HFA struct results and an indirect `sret` case, and callers that build each coerced value from memory; RUN lines for aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil and aarch64-pc-windows-msvc-msil at -O0 and -O2 with `-asm-verbose=false -verify-machineinstrs`, a shared `SIG:` prefix for every method header, CHECK lines for the call-site packing (`ldind.u2`, `ldind.u1`, `ldc.i4.s 16`, `shl`, `or`, then `call void 'g3'(int32)`), `--implicit-check-not=int64`, and `%if ilasm %{ ... %ilasm-verify -arm64 ... %}`. Execution stays x86_64-only (D6). (2) Item 6, `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/cc1as-unsupported.s` (new; text hooks-artifacts/cc1as-unsupported.s, spec hooks.md section 3): `# REQUIRES: msil-registered-target`; `not %clang -cc1as -triple <t> -filetype asm|null %s ... 2>&1 | FileCheck %s --implicit-check-not="PLEASE submit" --implicit-check-not="Stack dump"` for x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil and x86_64-pc-windows-msvc-msil (`-filetype asm -o -`) and aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil and aarch64-w64-windows-gnu-msil (`-filetype null`), `# CHECK: error in backend: assembly parsing is not supported for MSIL`, a native control `%if x86-registered-target %{ %clang -cc1as -triple x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu -filetype null %s %}`, body `nop`; never `-filetype obj` (an upstream-generic assertion for targets without an MCAsmBackend). Without the hook the assembly parser dereferences a null platform parser. (3) Item 10, `clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/mllvm.c` (new, `// REQUIRES: msil-registered-target`; prototype legs-proto/mllvm.c, spec legs.md item 10): `-mllvm -msil-assembly-name=foo` gives `.assembly 'foo'` (default `.assembly 'mllvm'`) through `%clang_cc1 -triple x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil`, `%clang --target=x86_64-pc-windows-msvc-msil` and `%clang --target=aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil`, and the last of two occurrences wins; `-###` shows `"-cc1" {{.*}}"-triple" "x86_64-pc-windows-msvc{{[0-9.]*}}-msil"` and then `"-mllvm" "-msil-assembly-name=foo"`; `not %clang_cc1 ... -mllvm -msil-no-such-option` reports 'Unknown command line argument'; global pass-manager options (`-print-after=msil-reg-stackify`, `-debug-pass=Structure` listing CodeGen Prepare, MSIL Instruction Selection, MSIL Register Stackify and MSIL Assembly Printer in order); per-context CodeGen options (`-print-after-isel` with `ARGUMENT_I32`, `-disable-cgp` through the driver with no 'CodeGen Prepare', `-verify-machineinstrs` with 'Verify generated machine code' after 'MSIL Register Stackify'); and a per-context IR-library option (`-O1` with and without `-mllvm -instcombine-code-sinking=0` on `void g(int c, int a, int b) { int d = a / b; if (c) use(d); }` with `use` defined through a volatile store: brfalse before div by default, div before brfalse with the option). Do not use `-stop-after` (item 4) or `-stackmap-version` (MSIL emits no stack maps), and do not count LowerInvoke (item 5 belongs to M11.b). Gate step 13 fails if one of the four files is missing; M11.d's manifest also names cc1as-unsupported.s as the fork test of llvm/lib/MC/MCParser/AsmParser.cpp.

#### M11.d Upstream-hooks check, hooks compile coverage, action pins, gate and CI wiring (infra, ~900 lines)

- Owned files: `msil/utils/upstream-hooks.txt`, `msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1`, `msil/utils/ci/check-upstream-hooks.py`, `msil/utils/ci/check-action-pins.py`, `msil/utils/ci/build.ps1`, `msil/utils/ci/build.sh`, `msil/utils/run-m1-gate.ps1`, `.github/workflows/msil-ci.yml`
- Depends on: M11.e
- Reads: llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/hooks.md; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/hooks-artifacts/ (draft manifest upstream-hooks.txt, check_manifest.py, proto-hooks-compile.ps1, configure-hooks.ps1, gen_litroot.py, curated.py, curated-paths.txt, run_curated.py); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs.md (item 2); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs-proto/check-action-pins.py; llvm/utils/lit/lit/reports.py (JsonReport, the format of lit's `-o` file); llvm/utils/lit/lit/cl_arguments.py (`fromfile_prefix_chars='@'`); llvm/unittests/MC/Disassembler.cpp (GTEST_SKIP for targets the build lacks); clang/test/Driver/spirv-openmp-toolchain.c; msil/utils/ci/ci-common.ps1; msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.ps1; msil/utils/ci/unit-tests.ps1; msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1; msil/utils/build/msil-syntax.ps1; msil/utils/build/msil-gtest.ps1; msil/utils/build/msil-lock.ps1; msil/utils/build/msil-configure.ps1; msil/utils/build/msil-build.ps1; msil/cmake/caches/MSIL.cmake; msil/utils/plan-check.py (exit-code and self-test conventions); this plan sections 2, 4 and 11, the M10 gate and the section 12 row 'Upstream merges ... also maintain msil/utils/upstream-hooks.txt'
- Provides: `python msil/utils/ci/check-upstream-hooks.py [--manifest <file>] [--repo <dir>] (--check | --list-lit | --lit-verdict <lit JSON report> [--min-pass N] | --self-test)` (exit 0 OK, 1 check failed, 2 usage error or malformed manifest); `pwsh -File msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1 [-BuildDir <dir>] [-HooksBuildDir <dir>] [-Leg manifest,pins,lit,gtest,compile] [-NoManifest] [-NoCompile] [-MinPass N] [-SelfTest]` (rows of step 'upstream-hooks'); `python msil/utils/ci/check-action-pins.py <repo>`; run-m1-gate.ps1 step 'upstream-hooks' and switch `-SkipUpstreamHooks`; msil-ci.yml Windows step 'Upstream hook tests'

Items 2, 6 (compile coverage) and 7, and the wiring of the new checks into the gate script and CI. Spawned after M11.e hands over, so its run-m1-gate.ps1 check sees the final leg scripts. (1) The manifest `msil/utils/upstream-hooks.txt`, from the draft hooks-artifacts/upstream-hooks.txt (format in its header): one `hook <path>` block for each of the 36 upstream files the fork changes (hooks.md section 1.1), each with at least one of `lit <path-or-glob>`, `gtest <exe> [<filter>]`, `compile`, `utc <test>...` (documentation only: M11.e's utc leg runs them) or `none <reason>`, plus `fork <test>` lines naming the fork tests that pin the MSIL side (for llvm/lib/MC/MCParser/AsmParser.cpp: clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/cc1as-unsupported.s, which M11.c writes); top-level `fork-only <path>` lines for fork files in upstream directories (.github/workflows/msil-ci.yml, clang/lib/Basic/Targets/MSIL.h, llvm/include/llvm/MC/MCSectionMSIL.h, clang/test/Driver/msil-toolchain.c, clang/test/Preprocessor/init-msil.c and the three msil-basic files of update_llc_test_checks), `switch-default <path>` (llvm/lib/MC/MCAsmBackend.cpp, llvm/lib/ExecutionEngine/JITLink/JITLink.cpp), `switch-other <path> <reason>` (llvm/lib/MC/StringTableBuilder.cpp: its `case XCOFF:` belongs to StringTableBuilder::Kind, not to an object-format enum), `allow <test> <reason>` (empty at introduction; only a per-file environment failure whose reason names the missing tool, never a directory, a glob or a real mismatch) and `min-pass 280`. The lit lines expand to 301 inputs: the 298 of hooks-artifacts/curated-paths.txt (the draft manifest lacks `lit clang/test/Frontend/embed-bitcode.ll`; add it to the llvm/lib/Bitcode/Writer/BitcodeWriter.cpp block), clang/test/Driver/cl-options.c and clang/test/Driver/clang_f_opts.c under clang/lib/Driver/ToolChains/Clang.cpp (the only upstream tests of its /GS and -fms-volatile hunks; they need llvm-profdata, which M11 adds to the build targets), and clang/test/Driver/spirv-openmp-toolchain.c under clang/lib/Driver/ToolChain.cpp (the curated list never reaches getLinkerWrapper, the one hunk there that changes control flow for native triples; this test builds a clang-linker-wrapper job, has no REQUIRES line and passed here in 2 s during planning). `--list-lit` must print exactly those 301 paths. Leave out the other 10 tests of hooks.md section 2.1 that need tools this build lacks (llvm-lto2, llvm-readobj, llvm-nm, llvm-objdump, llvm-mc, llvm-dwarfdump, llvm-objcopy) and the slow fsanitize.c, init.c and predefined-arch-macros.c (cheaper siblings cover their hooks). The manifest header documents the format, the base resolution of (2) and that the lit leg passes the list through a response file. (2) `msil/utils/ci/check-upstream-hooks.py` (Python standard library, from the prototype check_manifest.py). `--check`: the base is, in this order, `$env:MSIL_UPSTREAM_BASE`; `MERGE_HEAD` when `git rev-parse -q --verify MERGE_HEAD` succeeds (an upstream merge in progress, which the merge workflow gates before it commits; the diff against MERGE_HEAD is then exactly the fork's footprint on the new upstream); else the second parent of `git log --first-parent --merges -1 --grep='^\[MSIL\] Merge upstream llvm-project main at ' --format=%P HEAD`. It prints the base it chose and why; finding none (for example in a shallow clone) is an error that names the reason. The changed paths are `git diff --name-only <base>` (against the working tree) plus `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard`, so a milestone's uncommitted work counts; every changed path outside the fork-only prefixes (llvm/lib/Target/MSIL/, llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/, llvm/docs/MSIL/, llvm/unittests/Target/MSIL/, clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/, msil/, .github/msil/) and not marked fork-only needs a block; a block or fork-only line for an unchanged path is stale; every lit glob must match and every fork test must exist; an `allow` line is rejected when its test contains '*' or '?', names a directory or a path that is not one of the lit inputs, or has an empty reason; and every file that `git grep -l -E` finds in the whole monorepo (the exhaustive object-format switches, also in unbuilt projects such as lld or bolt) for `case (llvm::)?Triple::XCOFF *:`, `case (llvm::)?MCContext::IsXCOFF *:` or the unqualified `case +(XCOFF|IsXCOFF) *:([^:]|$)` (class scope or `using enum`, as llvm/lib/TargetParser/Triple.cpp:434 is written) must name MSIL or be switch-default or switch-other. `--list-lit` prints the expanded lit inputs, one per line. `--lit-verdict <file>` reads lit's JSON report (`-o`; one `code` per executed test, test names such as 'Clang :: Driver/cl-options.c', which it maps to repository paths such as clang/test/Driver/cl-options.c) and passes when every FAIL is allow-listed, no allow-listed test PASSes (a stale entry), no test is UNRESOLVED, TIMEOUT or XPASS, and the number of PASS results (XFAIL does not count) is at least the manifest's min-pass or `--min-pass`; it prints one line per problem and the counts. `--self-test` uses a temporary directory outside the repository (removed afterwards) and must report: a planted unlisted hook, a stale block, a missing fork test, a lit glob without a match, a qualified and an unqualified switch file without an MSIL case, allow lines with a glob, a directory and an empty reason, the MERGE_HEAD base during a merge in progress (a temporary git repository, or an injectable git runner), and the lit verdicts of synthetic JSON reports (FAIL for an unlisted failure, a stale allow entry, an UNRESOLVED result, an XPASS result and a PASS count below min-pass; PASS for an allow-listed failure). Exit codes as plan-check.py (0, 1, 2). (3) `msil/utils/ci/check-action-pins.py` (item 2; Python standard library, from legs-proto/check-action-pins.py): for every `uses: <owner>/<repo>[/<path>]@<ref> # <version>` line of `.github/**/msil-*.y*ml` it compares ref and version with what the other .github YAML files use for the same action, prints `<file>:<line>: ...; upstream: <ref> # <ver> (<n> uses)` per mismatch and exits 1 on any; actions that upstream never uses (actions/setup-dotnet) are reported without failing. (4) `msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1`, rows through ci-common.ps1 (step 'upstream-hooks'), legs in this order: `manifest` (check-upstream-hooks.py --check); `pins` (check-action-pins.py on the repository); `lit` (one msil-lit.ps1 call, as lit-legs.ps1 makes it: the first path of --list-lit is the mandatory path argument and the others go to `<build>/lit-results/upstream-hooks.rsp`, one per line, passed as `@<that file>` inside the -LitArgs value, so the list never meets the 32,767-character Windows command-line limit (about 19,300 characters as absolute paths today, and the list grows with every merge); the value is written `-LitArgs:-sv @<rsp> -o <build>/lit-results/upstream-hooks.json --xunit-xml-output=<build>/lit-results/upstream-hooks.xml` because it starts with '-' and contains a drive path, and the leg fails with a clear message if the build path contains a blank, which msil-lit.ps1 would split; log next to them; the verdict is `check-upstream-hooks.py --lit-verdict` on the JSON file, with `-MinPass` passed on as `--min-pass`); `gtest` (msil-gtest.ps1 -NoSkip on unittests/TargetParser/TargetParserTests.exe with `-Filter '*-HostTest.getMacOSHostVersion:HostTest.AIXHostVersionDetect:HostTest.AIXTargetVersionDetect:HostTest.AIXHostCPUDetect' -MinTests 700` and on unittests/MC/MCTests.exe with `-Filter '*-TargetRegistry.MSILLookupWithoutTarget<more>' -MinTests 40`, where `<more>` comes from `<build>/bin/llvm-config --targets-built`: `:Disassembler.WebAssemblyTest` when WebAssembly is not built (the CI build, msil/cmake/caches/MSIL.cmake, has X86 only) and `:Disassembler.X86Test` when X86 is not, because those tests call GTEST_SKIP without their target and -NoSkip fails a skip; the leading '*' keeps 'pwsh -File' from reading a value that starts with '-' and contains ':' as a parameter name); `compile` (item 6: in the private tree `-HooksBuildDir`, default `$env:MSIL_HOOKS_BUILD_DIR`, else `C:\llvm-msil-hooks-build`. The leg takes that tree's exclusive lock itself (`Enter-MsilBuildLock -BuildDir <tree>`) and, while it holds it, runs cmake and ninja directly; it never calls msil-configure.ps1 or msil-build.ps1, which take the same lock themselves and would wait for their own parent for up to 240 minutes. When the tree has no build.ninja, it configures it with the cmake options of hooks.md section 4.1 (projects clang;lldb, LLVM_TARGETS_TO_BUILD=ARM, no experimental targets or external projects, tests, docs, benchmarks and examples off, every optional LLDB dependency off, and the compilers, Python and job limits of msil-configure.ps1); then `ninja -C <tree> lib\Target\ARM\AsmParser\CMakeFiles\LLVMARMAsmParser.dir\ARMAsmParser.cpp.obj`, which also generates the ARM .inc files; then on every run `msil-syntax.ps1 -BuildDir <tree> -ExtraFlags /clang:-Werror=switch` (no lock of its own) on llvm/lib/Target/ARM/AsmParser/ARMAsmParser.cpp and lldb/source/Plugins/Process/gdb-remote/GDBRemoteCommunicationClient.cpp. Never build the lldb object with ninja (its order-only edges pull in about 1,000 unrelated TUs, 19 min), never delete or clean the tree (a broken cache takes `cmake --fresh` or a new `-HooksBuildDir`), never touch D:\llvm-msil\build). Switches: `-Leg`; `-NoManifest` and `-NoCompile` print a SKIP line for their leg and write no row; `-MinPass` overrides the manifest's min-pass; `-SelfTest` runs check-upstream-hooks.py --self-test, the MCTests filter derivation for the target lists 'X86' (excludes Disassembler.WebAssemblyTest) and 'X86 WebAssembly' (excludes neither), and a mutation check of the compile leg: copies of both TUs with their MSIL case line removed, under `[IO.Path]::GetTempPath()` and removed afterwards, must fail `msil-syntax.ps1 -BuildDir <hooks tree> -FlagsFrom <the original TU> -ExtraInclude <its source directory> -ExtraFlags /clang:-Werror=switch <copy>` with 'not handled in switch', and the originals must pass (the hooks tree must exist: without build.ninja the self-test fails and says to run the compile leg first); no self-test file is ever written inside the repository. Exit 1 if any row failed. The header documents the legs, that the script is Windows-only, and the costs: manifest ~11 s, pins ~1 s, lit ~1 min, gtest 10-25 s, compile ~47 s in steady state, ~13 min for a new tree and 5-13 min after an upstream merge; the existing tree is about 460 MB (the recon also built the lldb object there), about 140 MB when recreated by the script. (5) `msil/utils/run-m1-gate.ps1`: a new step 5 'upstream-hooks' after lit-legs (ci-sh becomes step 6) that runs upstream-hooks.ps1 with every leg when configure and build passed (a failed configure or build skips it, as it skips the unit tests and the lit legs), then checks the rows manifest, pins, lit, gtest and compile with Test-ChildRows; a `-SkipUpstreamHooks` switch like `-SkipUnitTests`; the header names the new lit legs utc and more-levels (M11.e) and says that the format and scope checks are M11 gate steps 25 and 26. (6) `.github/workflows/msil-ci.yml`: lines 179 and 289 pin `actions/upload-artifact@cf430e030ddbb5b0abf93d22962f4752f3646cd9 # v7.0.2` (upstream's pin since d770860630c7); the Windows job's Build step gets `id: build`, and a new step 'Upstream hook tests (msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1)' after the test legs runs `pwsh -NoProfile -File msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1 -NoCompile -NoManifest -MinPass 250` with `if: ${{ !cancelled() && steps.build.outcome == 'success' }}`, so it runs after failed unit tests or test legs, as run-m1-gate.ps1 does, and is skipped only when the build failed. Its comment says why: the checkout is shallow (no merge commit for the manifest leg), the hooks tree is a development-host cost, and the CI build has no WebAssembly target, so the list's WebAssembly tests are UNSUPPORTED (hence the lower -MinPass; the development host's gate enforces the manifest's min-pass) and Disassembler.WebAssemblyTest is filtered out. The existing upload step already keeps build/lit-results/ (upstream-hooks.json, .xml, .log). The header comment names the new legs and this step; triggers, guards and the other pins stay as they are. (7) `msil/utils/ci/build.ps1` and `msil/utils/ci/build.sh`: `llvm-profdata` joins the ninja targets (the two scripts keep the same list), and their comments name the M11 build targets. Exception to plan section 1.3 for this WP only: it may run cmake and ninja in the private hooks tree on C: (the recon left it configured, with the ARM object built), never in D:\llvm-msil\build. Checks: check-upstream-hooks.py --self-test and --check (until M11.c hands over, the only expected problem is the missing cc1as-unsupported.s), and `--list-lit` against curated-paths.txt plus the three added tests (301 lines); `upstream-hooks.ps1 -Leg manifest,pins,gtest,compile` and `-SelfTest`, with `git status --short` unchanged afterwards; `<build>/bin/llvm-config --targets-built` and the MCTests filter the script derives from it; the lit leg through msil-lit.ps1 exactly as the gate runs it (no recon helper): against the shared build everything passes except cl-options.c and clang_f_opts.c, which fail with exit status 127 until the gate builds llvm-profdata; validate those two before hand-over in a private lit root (hooks-artifacts/gen_litroot.py) whose llvm_tools_dir and clang_tools_dir point to a scratch directory holding hard links to D:\llvm-msil\build\bin plus 'C:/Program Files/Microsoft Visual Studio/18/Professional/VC/Tools/Llvm/x64/bin/llvm-profdata.exe' (both passed that way in about 17 s during planning), and record the result; the exact CI command `upstream-hooks.ps1 -NoCompile -NoManifest -MinPass 250` (exit 0, SKIP lines for manifest and compile, rows only for pins, lit and gtest); check-action-pins.py on the repository (0 mismatches after the bump) and on a scratch copy with one pin changed back (exit 1); `python -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(...)"` on msil-ci.yml; `run-m1-gate.ps1 -SkipConfigure -SkipBuild -SkipUnitTests -NoWsl -Leg expected` (shows the upstream-hooks rows).

#### M11.e CI legs: utc and more-levels (infra, ~150 lines)

- Owned files: `msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.ps1`, `msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.sh`
- Reads: llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs.md (items 8 and 9); llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs-proto/lit-legs.ps1.utc.diff; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs-proto/lit-legs.sh.utc.diff; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/hooks-artifacts/gen_litroot.py (private lit root); msil/utils/run-utc-tests.py; msil/utils/run-m1-gate.ps1 (Get-ExpectedLegs and the ci-sh header check); msil/utils/ci/ci-common.sh; msil/test/lit.cfg.py (msil_opt_levels, msil_runner); msil/cmake/caches/MSIL.cmake; this plan section 12 rows 'M5.b run-utc-tests.py contract amended' and 'Deferred after the upstream merge'
- Provides: legs `utc` and `more-levels` of lit-legs.ps1 and lit-legs.sh (default legs, rows 'lit-legs/utc' and 'lit-legs/more-levels')

Items 8 and 9, in both leg scripts (prototype diffs legs-proto/lit-legs.ps1.utc.diff and lit-legs.sh.utc.diff). (1) A non-lit leg `utc` after `expected`: `run-utc-tests.py --llc <build>/bin/llc[.exe] msil-basic x86-basic x86-mir-only x86-asm-mir-mixed x86-constant-pool asm-show-inst` (never wasm-function-name: the CI build has no WebAssembly target, and run-utc-tests.py fails a test whose target is missing instead of skipping it); in lit-legs.ps1 under the shared build lock as Invoke-ExpectedLeg does (`Invoke-UtcLeg`, dispatched like the expected leg), in lit-legs.sh through `python3` (`run_utc_leg`); log `<build>/lit-results/utc.log`, detail 'run-utc-tests.py: <the "N of M tests passed" line, or 'no summary'> (exit <rc>)', PASS when the exit status is 0. (2) A default (not -Full) lit leg `more-levels`: msil/test/M1 with `msil_opt_levels=O1,O3,Os,Oz`, after net8-tc0 in lit-legs.ps1 (before the -Wsl legs) and after net10-tc0 in lit-legs.sh (O0 and O2 run in the other legs; .ll tests run only O1 and O3, lit.cfg.py). Keep all-levels (-Full/--full, msil/test at all six levels) unchanged for nightly runs; no -Wsl variant (gate step 22 covers the Linux triple at these levels once in M11, and the Linux CI job on every run). (3) Headers: a line per new leg in the 'Legs, in order' block of both scripts, naming the M11 gate step that runs the same command (utc: step 20, more-levels: step 21; the M10 step numbers 5-12 of the other legs are unchanged in M11); lit-legs.sh's header must equal `lit-legs.sh --list` (run-m1-gate.ps1's ci-sh row compares them, and gate step 23 requires both new names in that list). run-m1-gate.ps1 needs no change for the legs (its expected rows come from `lit-legs.ps1 -List`), and msil-ci.yml none (it runs the leg scripts). Checks: `lit-legs.ps1 -List` (utc after expected, more-levels untagged); `lit-legs.ps1 -Leg utc` (PASS, 6 of 6); `lit-legs.ps1 -BuildDir <empty scratch dir> -Leg utc` (FAIL row, exit 1); in WSL `bash -n msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.sh`, `bash msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.sh --list` (equal to the header) and `--leg utc <empty dir>` (FAIL row, non-zero exit); the Linux code path of the utc leg once, `wsl -d Ubuntu -- python3 /mnt/d/llvm-msil/msil/utils/run-utc-tests.py --llc /mnt/d/llvm-msil/build/bin/llc.exe msil-basic x86-basic x86-mir-only x86-asm-mir-mixed x86-constant-pool asm-show-inst` (the Windows llc through WSL interop; 2 of 2 passed for the first two tests during planning). Before hand-over, run the Linux CI job's more-levels combination once, which no gate or recon has run: msil/test/M1 with `msil_runner=wsl`, `msil_triple=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil`, `msil_opt_levels=O1,O3,Os,Oz`, `msil_require_verify=1` and `msil_require_exec=1` through a private lit root (gen_litroot.py, or a private site config with msil_obj_root in scratch as legs.md item 9 did), so no shared Output directory is touched (12-15 min); report every failure with its reproducer (a compiler bug goes through a fix-loop grant, section 1.7a). Do not run the more-levels leg itself against the shared build's Output directories while other agents run lit (6-10 min; the gate runs it).

#### M11.f Documentation (docs, ~300 lines)

- Owned files: `llvm/docs/MSIL/GettingStarted.md`, `llvm/docs/MSIL/Status.md`, `msil/README.md`, `msil/test/README.md`, `msil/utils/build/README.md`
- Depends on: M11.a, M11.b, M11.c, M11.d, M11.e
- Reads: this plan section 6 M11 and the section 12 rows dated 2026-10-19 and 2026-10-20; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/split.md section 10; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/hooks.md sections 4 and 5; llvm/docs/MSIL/recon/m11/legs.md (summary for the drafter); llvm/docs/MSIL/GettingStarted.md sections 2, 7 and 8; llvm/docs/MSIL/Status.md sections 1-3; msil/README.md (Layout table); msil/utils/build/README.md (lock table, msil-build.ps1 example)

Written after the other WPs, from their hand-overs and the files they changed. GettingStarted.md section 2: the build command lists the M11 build targets (adds `llvm-profdata` and `TargetParserTests`). Section 7: rows `utc` (not lit: run-utc-tests.py with the six tests; lit reports llvm/test/tools/UpdateTestChecks UNSUPPORTED on Windows) and `more-levels` (msil/test/M1, `msil_opt_levels=O1,O3,Os,Oz`) in the leg table, and in 'The milestone-1 gate and CI' the upstream-hooks step: what each leg checks, the manifest msil/utils/upstream-hooks.txt and how to extend it after an upstream merge or a new hook (the base: `MSIL_UPSTREAM_BASE`, else MERGE_HEAD during a merge, else the last '[MSIL] Merge upstream llvm-project main at <sha>' commit, whose subject merges keep), the allow-list policy, the private hooks tree on C: (`MSIL_HOOKS_BUILD_DIR`, first-run and steady-state cost, never deleted by the scripts), `-NoCompile`, `-NoManifest`, `-MinPass` and `-SelfTest`, check-action-pins.py, run-m1-gate.ps1's `-SkipUpstreamHooks`, and its relation to lit-legs.sh's `--full` 'upstream' leg (the curated upstream-hooks check runs on every gate and CI run; the full `ninja check-llvm check-clang` leg stays for nightly Linux CI); section 8: `llc -stop-after=` / `-stop-before=` work at every MSIL pass from msil-isel on. Status.md: the state line under the title (after M11, 2026-10-20, branch msil, upstream llvm-project 64f50ffd4f1c merged); a section 3 row 'Basic block sections, late function splitting, basic block address map, static data partitioning (`-fbasic-block-sections=`, `-fsplit-machine-functions`, `-fbasic-block-address-map`, `-fpartition-static-data-sections`)': `-fsplit-machine-functions`, `-fbasic-block-sections=all` and `=list=`, `-fbasic-block-address-map` and `-fpartition-static-data-sections` are a driver error because MSIL is not ELF; `-fbasic-block-sections=none` is accepted, and `-fbasic-block-sections=labels` (a deprecated alias) passes the driver as `-fbasic-block-address-map` and ends in the backend's warning 'basic block address map is not supported for MSIL; ignored'; passed to cc1 or llc directly the options are ignored, the address map with that warning; never planned, because CIL methods have no sections; in 'Triples (D6)' the aarch64 note (since upstream de7794fcf781, aggregates under 8 bytes are passed as iN of their size, so 1-4-byte ones are int32 IL parameters; pinned by aarch64-aggregate-abi.c and struct-args-aapcs64.ll; execution stays x86_64); section 1: the first row's sentence that each test runs 'at `-O0` and `-O2`' now says that the gate also runs every execution test of msil/test/M1 at -O1, -O3, -Os and -Oz (the more-levels leg; .ll tests at -O1 and -O3), and the gate runs the upstream regression tests of the hooked upstream files; the test counts recounted from the tree (llc and MIR tests in llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL, clang tests in clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL, execution tests in msil/test/M1). msil/README.md 'Layout' table: a row for `utils/upstream-hooks.txt` (the manifest of hooked upstream files and their upstream tests), and the `utils/ci/` row names the upstream-hooks check (`upstream-hooks.ps1`, `check-upstream-hooks.py`) and the action-pin check (`check-action-pins.py`) besides configure, build, unit tests and lit legs. msil/test/README.md 'What runs': the more-levels leg (four more levels of M1 in every gate and CI run) and the utc leg. msil/utils/build/README.md: the msil-build.ps1 example lists the M11 build targets; a short section on the private hooks tree (its own exclusive lock through msil-lock.ps1, taken by upstream-hooks.ps1 itself, which runs cmake and ninja there directly; `msil-syntax.ps1 -BuildDir <tree>`; never deleted by scripts) and its row in the lock table. Design.md stays untouched.

**Build targets:** `llc` `opt` `clang` `FileCheck` `not` `count` `split-file` `llvm-config` `llvm-profdata` `TargetParserTests` `MCTests` `MSILTests`

**Gate commands** (run in order by the gate agent; all must succeed):

```text
 1. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-build.ps1 llc opt clang FileCheck not count split-file llvm-config llvm-profdata TargetParserTests MCTests MSILTests
 2. D:/llvm-msil/build/unittests/Target/MSIL/MSILTests.exe
 3. D:/llvm-msil/build/unittests/TargetParser/TargetParserTests.exe --gtest_filter=*MSIL*
 4. D:/llvm-msil/build/unittests/MC/MCTests.exe --gtest_filter=TargetRegistry.*
 5. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_native_oracle=required --param msil_run_netfx=1" llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL clang/test/Preprocessor/init-msil.c clang/test/Driver/msil-toolchain.c msil/test
 6. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_dotnet_runtime=10" msil/test/M1
 7. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_dotnet_env=DOTNET_TieredCompilation=0" msil/test/M1  (Windows .NET 8, optimizing JIT)
 8. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_runner=wsl --param msil_triple=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil" msil/test/M1 msil/test/harness  (WSL .NET 10; the harness path is the M10 step 8 amendment)
 9. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_runner=wsl --param msil_triple=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil --param msil_dotnet_env=DOTNET_TieredCompilation=0" msil/test/M1  (WSL .NET 10, optimizing JIT)
10. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs " -sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_runner=wsl --param msil_triple=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil --param msil_wsl_ilasm=D:/packages/NuGet/runtime.linux-x64.microsoft.netcore.ilasm/10.0.12/runtimes/linux-x64/native/ilasm" msil/test/M1/11-programs  (Linux ilasm inside WSL; the leading space inside -LitArgs is the M10 step 10 amendment)
11. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs " -sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_ilasm=D:/packages/NuGet/runtime.win-x64.microsoft.netcore.ilasm/10.0.12/runtimes/win-x64/native/ilasm.exe" msil/test/M1/11-programs  (modern ilasm on Windows; leading space as in the M10 step 11 amendment)
12. pwsh -NoProfile -Command ". D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-env.ps1; python D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/update-expected.py --clang D:/llvm-msil/build/bin/clang.exe --check D:/llvm-msil/msil/test/M1"
13. pwsh -NoProfile -Command '$r = "D:/llvm-msil/"; $req = [ordered]@{ "llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/function-splitting.ll" = "function-splitting=all"; "llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/stop-after-isel.ll" = "DBG_VALUE"; "llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/pipeline.ll" = "implicit-check-not=.Lower invoke and unwind"; "llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/struct-args-aapcs64.ll" = "aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil"; "clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/split-machine-functions.c" = "fsplit-machine-functions"; "clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/aarch64-aggregate-abi.c" = "aarch64-pc-windows-msvc-msil"; "clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/cc1as-unsupported.s" = "cc1as"; "clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/mllvm.c" = "msil-assembly-name"; "clang/test/Driver/msil-toolchain.c" = "fsplit-machine-functions" }; $bad = @(foreach ($k in $req.Keys) { $p = $r + $k; if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $p)) { "missing: $k" } elseif (-not (Select-String -Quiet -LiteralPath $p -Pattern $req[$k])) { "no match for $($req[$k]) in $k" } }); if ($bad.Count) { $bad; exit 1 }; "every M11 test is present"'  (lit only warns about a missing input path and still exits 0, so step 14 cannot notice a test that was never written)
14. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1" llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/function-splitting.ll llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/basic-block-sections.ll llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/stack-protector-warning.ll llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/stop-after-isel.ll llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/reg-argument.mir llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/pipeline.ll llvm/test/CodeGen/MSIL/struct-args-aapcs64.ll clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/split-machine-functions.c clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/aarch64-aggregate-abi.c clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/cc1as-unsupported.s clang/test/CodeGen/MSIL/mllvm.c clang/test/Driver/msil-toolchain.c  (the M11 tests of items 1, 3, 4, 5, 6 and 10, also inside step 5; about 2 min)
15. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1  (items 2, 6 and 7: legs manifest, pins, lit, gtest and compile; about 3 min, up to 15 min when the hooks tree on C: must be configured or rebuilt after an upstream merge)
16. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1 -SelfTest  (planted manifest, switch, allow-list, MERGE_HEAD and lit-verdict defects are reported; both hooked TUs fail the compile check without their MSIL case; the MCTests filter follows the built targets)
17. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/ci/upstream-hooks.ps1 -NoCompile -NoManifest -MinPass 250  (the exact command of the Windows CI step: SKIP lines for manifest and compile, rows for pins, lit and gtest)
18. python D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/ci/check-action-pins.py D:/llvm-msil  (item 2: every action in .github/workflows/msil-*.yml pinned as upstream pins it)
19. python -c "import yaml; yaml.safe_load(open('D:/llvm-msil/.github/workflows/msil-ci.yml', encoding='utf-8'))"  (msil-ci.yml still parses; it never runs locally)
20. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.ps1 -Leg utc  (item 8: run-utc-tests.py with its six tests; fails with "unknown leg" without M11.e)
21. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.ps1 -Leg more-levels  (item 9: msil/test/M1 at O1, O3, Os and Oz on .NET 8 with msil_require_verify=1 and msil_require_exec=1; fails with "unknown leg" without M11.e; 6-10 min)
22. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/build/msil-lit.ps1 -LitArgs "-sv --param msil_require_verify=1 --param msil_require_exec=1 --param msil_runner=wsl --param msil_triple=x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil --param msil_opt_levels=O1,O3,Os,Oz" msil/test/M1  (item 9 for the Linux CI job's triple, which runs more-levels natively: WSL .NET 10; 12-15 min)
23. pwsh -NoProfile -Command '$l = @(wsl -d Ubuntu --cd /mnt/d/llvm-msil --exec bash msil/utils/ci/lit-legs.sh --list); $rc = $LASTEXITCODE; $l; if ($rc -ne 0 -or $l -notcontains "utc" -or $l -notcontains "more-levels") { exit 1 }'  (items 8 and 9 in the Linux CI job's leg script)
24. pwsh -NoProfile -File D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/run-m1-gate.ps1  (runs the msil/utils/ci scripts in CI order and prints the summary; its rows now include lit-legs utc and more-levels and upstream-hooks manifest, pins, lit, gtest and compile)
25. pwsh -NoProfile -Command "Set-Location D:/llvm-msil; git add -A; python 'C:/Program Files/Microsoft Visual Studio/18/Professional/VC/Tools/Llvm/x64/bin/git-clang-format' --binary 'C:/Program Files/Microsoft Visual Studio/18/Professional/VC/Tools/Llvm/x64/bin/clang-format.exe' --extensions c,cpp,h,hpp,hxx,cxx,inc,cppm,cl --diff HEAD -- llvm/lib llvm/include llvm/unittests clang/lib clang/include"  (must print no diff; same extension list as llvm/utils/git/code-format-helper.py, so .td files are never formatted)
26. pwsh -NoProfile -Command "git -C D:/llvm-msil add -A; python D:/llvm-msil/msil/utils/plan-check.py scope M11"  (every staged path is owned by a WP of M11, is a M11 fix-loop amendment in section 12, or is on the allow-list)
```

**Exit criteria**
- Item 3: on both x86_64 MSIL triples, `llc -function-splitting=all` (also with -O0, `-mfs-split-ehcode`, `--basic-block-sections=all` or a profile, and `--basic-block-address-map`) and clang cc1 `-fsplit-machine-functions`, `-Xclang -fsplit-machine-functions` and `-mllvm -function-splitting=all` exit 0 with IL identical to the run without the option (only the existing address-map warning); `-debug-pass=Structure` shows no basic-block-sections pass; the driver still rejects the ELF-only section options on every MSIL triple and passes `-fbasic-block-sections=labels` on as the address map (function-splitting.ll, split-machine-functions.c, msil-toolchain.c).
- Item 4: `llc -stop-after=msil-isel` and `-stop-before=msil-argument-move` exit 0 on both triples at -O0 and -O2, the MIR reads back with `-run-pass=none`, and `-start-after=msil-isel` gives the full pipeline's IL, also for a function with debug info whose argument DBG_VALUEs follow the moved ARGUMENTs (stop-after-isel.ll); no MSIL test passes `-disable-mir-output-verify`; reg-argument.mir passes unchanged.
- Item 5: LowerInvoke runs once and UnreachableBlockElim twice in every MSIL pipeline, at the positions pipeline.ll pins; every existing invoke test (function-bitcasts.ll, call-errors.ll, frame-escape-errors.ll) passes unchanged.
- Items 1, 6 and 10: aarch64-aggregate-abi.c and struct-args-aapcs64.ll pin the aarch64 aggregate shapes in IR and IL on all four aarch64 MSIL triples and assemble with `-arm64`; cc1as-unsupported.s pins the IsMSIL assembly-parser hook (clean error, no crash); mllvm.c pins global and per-context `-mllvm` options through cc1 and the driver. Gate step 13 proves that every new test file exists.
- Items 6 and 7: upstream-hooks.ps1 passes with every leg: the manifest covers every upstream file the fork changes (36 hook blocks at introduction) and every exhaustive object-format switch, qualified or not, against the right base (MERGE_HEAD during a merge); the 301 curated upstream lit inputs give at least min-pass PASS results with an empty allow-list; the full TargetParserTests and MCTests pass (host-gated tests, and tests of targets the build lacks, excluded by name); ARMAsmParser.cpp and GDBRemoteCommunicationClient.cpp compile with `-Werror=switch` in the private tree; `-SelfTest` proves that each check, the allow-list rules and the lit verdict fail on a planted defect, and leaves the working tree unchanged. The residual gap is recorded (the lldb TU is syntax-checked only; no ARM or lldb test runs).
- Item 2: msil-ci.yml pins actions/upload-artifact at upstream's v7.0.2 SHA, check-action-pins.py reports 0 mismatches, and msil-ci.yml parses.
- Items 8 and 9: lit-legs.ps1 and lit-legs.sh have the default legs utc and more-levels (gate steps 20, 21 and 23); run-utc-tests.py passes its six tests; msil/test/M1 passes at O1, O3, Os and Oz on the Windows triple and, under WSL, on x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu-msil (gate step 22); run-m1-gate.ps1 and both CI jobs run the legs, and the Windows CI job also runs the upstream-hooks pins, lit and gtest legs whenever its build passed (step 17 runs that exact command).
- run-m1-gate.ps1 prints every row PASS, including the new rows; every M10 leg stays green; no IL changes for any input that compiled before M11.
- GettingStarted.md, Status.md (counts included), msil/README.md, msil/test/README.md and msil/utils/build/README.md describe the fixes, the new legs, the build targets and the upstream-hooks check.

**Review focus** (in addition to the three standing lenses)
- Items 3-5 change no IL: the function-splitting reset sits where llc, `-mllvm` and cc1 all reach it; the ARGUMENT move keeps DBG_VALUE placement and relative order; the early invoke lowering stays and nothing EH-related runs after CodeGenPrepare.
- Upstream idioms and minimal diffs: WebAssembly's post-fa24f6dba7cd shape for item 5, MSILArgumentMove kept for MIR input, no `-disable-mir-output-verify`, FailsVerification or reserved ARGUMENTS; no clang change.
- Each new test fails without its fix (items 3-5) or pins the upstream change it names (items 1, 6, 10); the curated upstream tests really exercise the hooked files; the allow-list is empty or narrow and justified per file; the manifest check cannot pass silently (shallow clone, stale entries, unmatched globs, unqualified switch cases, the base during a merge in progress); the lit verdict counts PASS only and fails on UNRESOLVED, XPASS and stale allow entries.
- The gate and CI scripts: the compile leg never deletes the hooks tree, never nests the tree's lock and never touches D:\llvm-msil\build; self-tests write nothing inside the repository; SKIP and FAIL rows are honest; CI's `-MinPass`, its gtest filter, its skipped legs and its `if:` guard are explained; lit-legs.sh's header matches its `--list`.

**Rebuild cost.** MSIL target: 15 TUs (MSILTargetMachine.cpp, MSILISelDAGToDAG.cpp and the 13 that include MSILUtilities.h, MSILArgumentMove.cpp and MSILUtilities.cpp among them), plus relinks of llc, opt, clang, MCTests and MSILTests (~10-20 min); llvm-profdata is new: llvm-profdata.cpp plus the two libraries the build lacks, LLVMDebuginfod and LLVMHTTP (6 TUs in all), and one link (est. 2-4 min, 30-60 MB on D:). The private hooks tree on C: already exists (about 460 MB; if it has to be recreated: 242 s configure plus 473 s for the ARM object, about 140 MB). The gate adds about 45-55 min to M10's ~2 h: step 13 seconds, step 14 ~2 min, steps 15-17 ~6 min, steps 18-20 under a minute, step 21 6-10 min, step 22 12-15 min, step 23 seconds, and step 24 grows by the more-levels, utc and upstream-hooks rows (~10-14 min).

## 7. Future milestones (phases 2-5)

Each becomes a sequence of small milestones with the same protocol when its phase starts. Every phase that needs widely-included header edits (MCSymbol kinds, IR intrinsics, RuntimeLibcalls.td, CallingConv.h, clang Attr.td) front-loads them into its first milestone, as M1 does.

### P2-A. CIL object files and the MC object writer

Phase 2 start: an MCSymbolMSIL with signatures, an MSIL MC object streamer and a CIL object format modeled on the wasm object format (method bodies in CIL, data segments, relocations, typed symbol table), the CIL binary encoder (opcode values checked against ECMA-335), and 'clang -c' producing .o. Requires a batched broad-rebuild header commit (MCSymbol kind if needed, MCContext createSymbolImpl, BinaryFormat additions), landed as the first milestone of the phase like M1.

### P2-B. msil-ld: lld flavor, symbol resolution, LTO and the PE writer

An lld flavor modeled on lld/wasm: loads objects and archives, runs LTO on bitcode (accepting stock-clang bitcode for the native triple after retargeting the module triple), resolves symbols to objects, managed assemblies and native libraries, lays out data and writes the PE/ECMA-335 metadata directly; ilasm remains a test oracle (round trip). The clang driver gains MSILToolChain<NativeTC> invoking msil-ld. Neither the driver nor msil-ld writes runtimeconfig.json or .exe.config: like Roslyn (csc), which writes only the assembly and its PDB, they leave them to the build system or the user. msil-ld is given reference assemblies (-r) and binds every BCL type and member the generated code needs (Marshal::AllocHGlobal, Interlocked::MemoryBarrier, Math, attributes such as DisableRuntimeMarshalling, ...) by searching them, referencing whichever assembly defines it (.NET 8 reference assemblies give references such as System.Runtime, System.Runtime.InteropServices and System.Threading, .NET Framework's give mscorlib); there is no core-library switch and no target-framework option (section 12, 2026-10-14).

### P2-C. Exports and assembly-to-assembly linking

-shared libraries with a public export class (--export-class), the symbol-table custom attribute carrying names, kinds, signatures and the triple, linking against managed assemblies built by this toolchain, and P/Invoke resolution from native export tables / import libraries (DT_SONAME, kernel32.lib style mapping).

### P3-A. Runtime support in the output assembly and codegen switches for newer .NET APIs

No runtime support assembly (section 12, 2026-10-15): the generated code calls the BCL directly wherever an API exists, and no output assembly needs a runtime support assembly: its only dependencies are the BCL, the native libraries it P/Invokes, and any assemblies it was explicitly linked against (P2-C) (the C math library stays a host-libc import, D14, apart from the specific APIs the switches below permit); process startup is the generated '<msil>Main' that Phase 1 already emits (D27, M3; argv from Environment.GetCommandLineArgs). P3-A covers the shadow stack for dynamic alloca and stacksave/restore and the TLS block for thread_local ([ThreadStatic] fields with NativeMemory or Marshal.AllocHGlobal), global destructors and atexit via AppDomain.ProcessExit, small helpers that the backend or msil-ld emits into the output assembly, private to it, where no suitable BCL API exists or the default must also run on .NET Framework 4.8 (IL generated by the toolchain, or C helpers compiled by this toolchain and linked statically by msil-ld, the way LLVM links compiler-rt builtins); individual, independent codegen switches (module flags or TargetOptions) that permit specific newer APIs or attributes, among them DisableRuntimeMarshalling, Math.FusedMultiplyAdd and UnmanagedCallersOnly thunks instead of delegate thunks (P3-B); and BCL intrinsics for bit ops, rounding, min/max and bitcasts (isel.md decision 11), each behind its switch (D16). Where that is possible, an API or attribute is also usable only if some reference assembly defines it (P2-B). The defaults keep the generated code running on .NET Framework 4.8 and modern .NET alike, as Phase 1 output does (which runtimes the output loads on depends on its references, P2-B), and what Design.md 7.1 (and 3.2 for SIMD) records about the differences decides which switches exist and their safe defaults. There is no profile: a build system may combine switches into "net8.0", but the compiler has no such concept (section 12, 2026-10-14).

### P3-B. Variadics, native-callable function pointers, setjmp/longjmp, atomics

Variadic definitions and calls (native va_list construction over an argument buffer, v-variant table, msil_fixed_arity attribute), native-callable thunks (kept-alive delegates by default, [UnmanagedCallersOnly] thunks behind their P3-A switch) with the in-assembly fast path, setjmp/longjmp via a BCL exception type that the setjmp caller catches with an IL exception filter matching the jmp_buf (from the Emscripten SjLj lowering; no exception type is shared between assemblies), C11 atomics mapped to Interlocked/Volatile, i128 division and f16 through Int128/UInt128 and System.Half where the codegen switches and references allow (P3-A) and through compiler-rt-style helpers in the output assembly otherwise, and such helpers also for C complex (__muldc3, __divdc3, ...; System.Numerics.Complex does not follow C Annex G's infinity and NaN rules) and for the other compiler-rt calls the legalizer emits where no suitable BCL API exists (i128 floating-point conversions, bfloat conversions, ...); glibc and UCRT host-libc conformance (zlib, Lua, SQLite).

### P4-A. Conformance and performance

GCC torture tests and llvm-test-suite per triple through the same transcript harness, benchmarks against native clang, IL-size-aware inlining, revisit the frame model (value-type frame local vs localloc, measured 73 vs 4 bytes of prologue), full SIMD (Vector128/256/512 and arch intrinsics), Portable PDB debug info, musl and Darwin triples (UserLabelPrefix fix for m:o), new pass manager pipeline.

### P5-A. Production toolchain

32-bit triples (i686-pc-windows-msvc-msil with stdcall/cdecl signatures and the ILP32 typing rule) and static host libcs where viable. Like LLVM itself, the project ships compiler tools only; build systems (CMake, MSBuild, ...) are separate projects that integrate the compiler (section 12, 2026-10-14).

Both runtimes resolve through `/usr/bin/dotnet`.
